ERCP completed test case document

Updated 6 October 2026. 270 automated cases, one k6 load scenario and one migration CLI check passed. Every suite and the load scenario were rerun on 6 October using the QMS setup. No tests were skipped. Each record is linked to actual tool evidence. Parameterized cases are separate records. Responsible reviewers follow the supplied module allocation; this is not a claim of individual execution authorship.

Source excerpts preserve fixture inputs, actions and assertions. Existing weak smoke tests are retained transparently; passing them alone does not establish feature coverage.

Steps
From the repository run dotnet test backend-tests/ERCP.Api.Tests.csproj --filter "FullyQualifiedName~ERCP.Api.Tests.AuthControllerTests.GenerateJwtToken_AllRoles_ValidatesAndContainsSingleRoleClaim". Use the inline parameter shown in the case name when applicable.
Expected
Assert.True(regResult.Success); Assert.NotEmpty(tokenString); Assert.Equal(2, roleClaims.Count); Assert.Equal(roleName, roleClaims[0].Value); Assert.NotNull(principal); Assert.NotNull(validatedToken); Assert.True(principal.IsInRole(roleName));
Actual
Passed; duration 00:00:00.8467105; recorded 2026-10-06T00:15:35.3745627+05:30
Evidence
evidence/final/backend/backend.trx
Fixture inputs and assertion source
public async Task GenerateJwtToken_AllRoles_ValidatesAndContainsSingleRoleClaim(string roleName)
    {
        // Arrange
        var context = GetInMemoryContext();
        var (_, authService, _) = CreateController(context);

        var regResult = await authService.RegisterAsync(new RegisterRequestDto
        {
            FullName = $"Test User {roleName}",
            Email = $"{roleName.ToLower()}@test.local",
            Password = "Password123!",
            Role = roleName
        });

        Assert.True(regResult.Success);
        var tokenString = regResult.Response!.Token;
        Assert.NotEmpty(tokenString);

        // Act - Read token claims using JwtSecurityTokenHandler
        var tokenHandler = new System.IdentityModel.Tokens.Jwt.JwtSecurityTokenHandler();
        var jwtToken = tokenHandler.ReadJwtToken(tokenString);

        // Assert role claim presence and uniqueness (two claims are added: standard ClaimTypes.Role and custom lowercase "role")
        var roleClaims = jwtToken.Claims.Where(c => c.Type == "role" || c.Type == ClaimTypes.Role).ToList();
        Assert.Equal(2, roleClaims.Count);
        Assert.Equal(roleName, roleClaims[0].Value);

        // Act & Assert - Validate token with TokenValidationParameters
        var validationParameters = new TokenValidationParameters
        {
            ValidateIssuer = true,
            ValidIssuer = "ERCP.Api",
            ValidateAudience = true,
            ValidAudience = "ERCP.Clients",
            ValidateLifetime = true,
            ValidateIssuerSigningKey = true,
            IssuerSigningKey = new SymmetricSecurityKey(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes("DEVELOPMENT_ONLY_SecretKey_MustBeChangedInProduction!!")),
            RoleClaimType = ClaimTypes.Role,
            NameClaimType = ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier
        };

        var principal = tokenHandler.ValidateToken(tokenString, validationParameters, out var validatedToken);
        Assert.NotNull(principal);
        Assert.NotNull(validatedToken);
        Assert.True(principal.IsInRole(roleName));
    }
}

Deneshkar. P

SEF/emergency-resource-coordination-platform/backend-tests/AuthControllerTests.cs
Preconditions
Restored .NET 8 test project; fixtures created by the named test. Existing controller tests mostly use EF InMemory.
Steps
From the repository run dotnet test backend-tests/ERCP.Api.Tests.csproj --filter "FullyQualifiedName~ERCP.Api.Tests.AuthControllerTests.GetProfile_WithAuthenticatedClaim_ReturnsUserProfile". Use the inline parameter shown in the case name when applicable.
Expected
var okResult = Assert.IsType<OkObjectResult>(profileResult.Result); var profile = Assert.IsType<UserProfileDto>(okResult.Value); Assert.Equal(userId, profile.Id); Assert.Equal("alice@example.com", profile.Email); Assert.Equal("Coordinator", profile.Role); Assert.Equal("Alice Walker", profile.FullName);
Actual
Passed; duration 00:00:00.0691899; recorded 2026-10-06T00:15:35.8751383+05:30

evidence/final/backend/backend.trx
Fixture inputs and assertion source
public async Task GetProfile_WithAuthenticatedClaim_ReturnsUserProfile()
    {
        // Arrange
        var context = GetInMemoryContext();
        var (controller, authService, _) = CreateController(context);

        var regResult = await authService.RegisterAsync(new RegisterRequestDto
        {
            FullName = "Alice Walker",
            Email = "alice@example.com",
            Password = "SecretPassword1!",
            Role = "Coordinator"
        });

        var userId = regResult.Response!.User.Id;

        // Mock HttpContext User claims
        var claims = new List<Claim>
        {
            new(ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier, userId.ToString()),
            new(ClaimTypes.Email, "alice@example.com"),
            new(ClaimTypes.Role, "Coordinator")
        };
        var identity = new ClaimsIdentity(claims, "TestAuth");
        controller.ControllerContext = new ControllerContext
        {
            HttpContext = new DefaultHttpContext { User = new ClaimsPrincipal(identity) }
        };

        // Act
        var profileResult = await controller.GetProfile();

        // Assert
        var okResult = Assert.IsType<OkObjectResult>(profileResult.Result);
        var profile = Assert.IsType<UserProfileDto>(okResult.Value);

        Assert.Equal(userId, profile.Id);
        Assert.Equal("alice@example.com", profile.Email);
        Assert.Equal("Coordinator", profile.Role);
        Assert.Equal("Alice Walker", profile.FullName);
    }

public async Task Login_WithIncorrectPassword_ReturnsUnauthorized()
    {
        // Arrange
        var context = GetInMemoryContext();
        var (controller, _, _) = CreateController(context);

        await controller.Register(new RegisterRequestDto
        {
            FullName = "Valid User",
            Email = "valid@test.com",
            Password = "CorrectPassword1!",
            Role = "Volunteer"
        });

        // Act
        var loginResult = await controller.Login(new LoginRequestDto
        {
            Email = "valid@test.com",
            Password = "WrongPassword!"
        });

        // Assert
        Assert.IsType<UnauthorizedObjectResult>(loginResult.Result);
    }

public async Task Login_WithValidCredentials_ReturnsOkWithJwt()
    {
        // Arrange
        var context = GetInMemoryContext();
        var (controller, authService, _) = CreateController(context);

        await authService.RegisterAsync(new RegisterRequestDto
        {
            FullName = "Jane Coordinator",
            Email = "coordinator@test.com",
            Password = "CoordSecret123!",
            Role = "Coordinator"
        });

        // Act
        var loginResult = await controller.Login(new LoginRequestDto
        {
            Email = "coordinator@test.com",
            Password = "CoordSecret123!"
        });

        // Assert
        var okResult = Assert.IsType<OkObjectResult>(loginResult.Result);
        var response = Assert.IsType<AuthResponseDto>(okResult.Value);

        Assert.NotEmpty(response.Token);
        Assert.Equal("Coordinator", response.User.Role);
        Assert.Equal("coordinator@test.com", response.User.Email);
    }

public async Task Register_DuplicateEmail_ReturnsConflict()
    {
        // Arrange
        var context = GetInMemoryContext();
        var (controller, _, _) = CreateController(context);

        var dto = new RegisterRequestDto
        {
            FullName = "First User",
            Email = "duplicate@example.com",
            Password = "Password123!",
            Role = "Citizen"
        };

        await controller.Register(dto);

        // Act - Register again with same email
        var duplicateDto = new RegisterRequestDto
        {
            FullName = "Second User",
            Email = "duplicate@example.com",
            Password = "Password456!",
            Role = "Volunteer"
        };
        var result = await controller.Register(duplicateDto);

        // Assert
        Assert.IsType<ConflictObjectResult>(result.Result);
    }

BE-011 ERCP.Api.Tests.DispatchAllocationTests.DeletingResourceClearsDispatchTaskResourceId

Passed

Responsible Reviewer
M.Thanusikan
Source
SEF/emergency-resource-coordination-platform/backend-tests/DispatchAllocationTests.cs
Preconditions
Restored .NET 8 test project; fixtures created by the named test. Existing controller tests mostly use EF InMemory.
Steps
From the repository run dotnet test backend-tests/ERCP.Api.Tests.csproj --filter "FullyQualifiedName~ERCP.Api.Tests.DispatchAllocationTests.DeletingResourceClearsDispatchTaskResourceId". Use the inline parameter shown in the case name when applicable.
Expected
Assert.Null(task.ResourceId);
Actual
Passed; duration 00:00:00.0098351; recorded 2026-10-06T00:15:35.3884293+05:30
Evidence
evidence/final/backend/backend.trx
Fixture inputs and assertion source
public async Task DeletingResourceClearsDispatchTaskResourceId()
    {
        using var context = CreateContext();
        var volunteer = new Volunteer { Name = "Responder", Email = "resource-link@test.local" };
        var resource = new Resource { Name = "Rescue Vehicle", ResourceType = "Vehicle" };
        var dispatch = new Dispatch { IncidentId = 17, Priority = "High" };
        context.Volunteers.Add(volunteer);
        context.Resources.Add(resource);
        context.Dispatches.Add(dispatch);
        await context.SaveChangesAsync();
        var task = new DispatchTask
        {
            DispatchId = dispatch.Id,
            VolunteerId = volunteer.Id,
            ResourceId = resource.Id,
            TaskType = "Transport",
        };
        context.DispatchTasks.Add(task);
        await context.SaveChangesAsync();

        context.Resources.Remove(resource);
        await context.SaveChangesAsync();

        Assert.Null(task.ResourceId);
    }
}

BE-012 ERCP.Api.Tests.DispatchAllocationTests.DispatchTask_ResourceRelationshipIsOptionalIntegerAndSetNullOnDelete

Responsible Reviewer

Preconditions
Restored .NET 8 test project; fixtures created by the named test. Existing controller tests mostly use EF InMemory.
Steps
From the repository run dotnet test backend-tests/ERCP.Api.Tests.csproj --filter "FullyQualifiedName~ERCP.Api.Tests.DispatchAllocationTests.DispatchTask_ResourceRelationshipIsOptionalIntegerAndSetNullOnDelete". Use the inline parameter shown in the case name when applicable.
Expected
Assert.Equal(typeof(int?), taskEntity.FindProperty(nameof(DispatchTask.ResourceId))!.ClrType); Assert.False(resourceForeignKey.IsRequired); Assert.Equal(DeleteBehavior.SetNull, resourceForeignKey.DeleteBehavior);
Actual
Passed; duration 00:00:00.0065845; recorded 2026-10-06T00:15:35.3729237+05:30
Evidence
evidence/final/backend/backend.trx
Fixture inputs and assertion source
public void DispatchTask_ResourceRelationshipIsOptionalIntegerAndSetNullOnDelete()
    {
        using var context = CreateContext();
        var taskEntity = context.Model.FindEntityType(typeof(DispatchTask))!;
        var resourceForeignKey = taskEntity.GetForeignKeys()
            .Single(foreignKey => foreignKey.PrincipalEntityType.ClrType == typeof(Resource));

        Assert.Equal(typeof(int?), taskEntity.FindProperty(nameof(DispatchTask.ResourceId))!.ClrType);
        Assert.False(resourceForeignKey.IsRequired);
        Assert.Equal(DeleteBehavior.SetNull, resourceForeignKey.DeleteBehavior);
    }

public async Task ProposeAllocation_PersistsPlanAndReturnsAllocationResults()
    {
        using var context = CreateContext();
        var dispatch = new Dispatch { IncidentId = 17, Priority = "High" };
        var plan = new IncidentPlan
        {
            IncidentId = 17,
            PlanTitle = "Fire response allocation",
            PlanDescription = "Deploy medical and transport support.",
            DispatchPlanJson = "[{\"Action\":\"Deploy ambulance\",\"Resource\":\"Ambulance 2\",\"Location\":\"Sector 4\",\"Reason\":\"Medical response\"}]",
            SuggestedAssignmentsJson = "[{\"Role\":\"Medic\",\"EntityName\":\"Ava Responder\",\"Reason\":\"Medical skills\"}]",
        };
        context.Dispatches.Add(dispatch);
        context.IncidentPlans.Add(plan);
        await context.SaveChangesAsync();

        var controller = new DispatchesController(context);
        var proposalResult = await controller.ProposeAllocation(
            dispatch.Id,
            new ProposeDispatchAllocationDto(plan.Id));

        Assert.IsType<OkObjectResult>(proposalResult);
        Assert.Equal(plan.Id, dispatch.AllocationPlanId);
        Assert.Equal("PendingApproval", dispatch.Status);

        var getResult = await controller.GetById(dispatch.Id);
        var response = Assert.IsType<DispatchResponseDto>(
            Assert.IsType<OkObjectResult>(getResult.Result).Value);
        Assert.NotNull(response.AllocationProposal);
        Assert.Equal("Fire response allocation", response.AllocationProposal.PlanTitle);
        Assert.Equal("Deploy ambulance", Assert.Single(response.AllocationProposal.DispatchPlan).Action);
        Assert.Equal("Ava Responder", Assert.Single(response.AllocationProposal.SuggestedAssignments).EntityName);
    }

public async Task ValidateDispatch_UnavailableResourceRejectsAllocation()

        var resource = new Resource
        {
            Name = "Ambulance 2",

        };

        context.Dispatches.Add(dispatch);
        await context.SaveChangesAsync();

        {

public async Task ValidateDispatch_EmitsTimelineEvent_WhenIncidentExists()
    {
        var ctx = GetInMemoryContext();
        var incident = new Incident
        {
            Title = "Timeline Incident",
            Description = "desc",
            Location = "Sector 1",
            Category = "Fire",
            Priority = "High",
            Status = "Reported",
        };
        ctx.Incidents.Add(incident);
        await ctx.SaveChangesAsync();

        var volunteer = new Volunteer
        {
            Name = "Timeline Responder",
            Email = "timeline@test.local",
            Phone = "0770000001",
            Skills = "medical",
            IsAvailable = true,
        };
        ctx.Volunteers.Add(volunteer);
        await ctx.SaveChangesAsync();

        var dispatch = new Dispatch { IncidentId = incident.Id, Priority = "High", Status = "Proposed" };
        ctx.Dispatches.Add(dispatch);
        await ctx.SaveChangesAsync();
        ctx.DispatchTasks.Add(new DispatchTask
        {
            DispatchId = dispatch.Id,
            VolunteerId = volunteer.Id,
            TaskType = "MedicalEvac",
            Status = "Assigned",
            AssignedAt = DateTime.UtcNow,
        });
        await ctx.SaveChangesAsync();

        var service = new DispatchValidationService(ctx);
        await service.ValidateDispatchAsync(dispatch.Id, new ValidateDispatchRequestDto(), "Coordinator");

        Assert.Single(ctx.IncidentTimelineEvents.Where(e => e.EventType == "ValidationPerformed"));
    }
}

public async Task ValidateDispatch_OverlappingShift_ReturnsRevise_WithV2()
    {
        var (ctx, dispatch, volunteer) = await SeedDispatchAsync(volunteerAvailable: true);

        var start = new DateTime(2026, 9, 20, 8, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc);
        var end = new DateTime(2026, 9, 20, 16, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc);
        ctx.Shifts.Add(new Shift
        {
            VolunteerId = volunteer.Id,
            StartTime = start,
            EndTime = end,
            Status = ShiftStatus.Scheduled,
        });
        await ctx.SaveChangesAsync();

        var service = new DispatchValidationService(ctx);
        var result = await service.ValidateDispatchAsync(dispatch.Id, new ValidateDispatchRequestDto(
            ProposedShiftStart: new DateTime(2026, 9, 20, 10, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc),
            ProposedShiftEnd: new DateTime(2026, 9, 20, 18, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc)));

        Assert.False(result.IsValid);
        Assert.Contains(result.Violations, v => v.Code == "V2_DOUBLE_BOOKING");
        // Double-booking alone is revisable (pick another volunteer/time)
        Assert.Equal("REVISE", result.Recommendation);
    }

public async Task UpdateStatus_ApprovalWithNewerFailedSafetyAudit_IsRejected()
    {
        using var context = CreateContext();
        var dispatch = new Dispatch { IncidentId = 1, Priority = "High", Status = "PendingApproval" };
        context.Dispatches.Add(dispatch);
        await context.SaveChangesAsync();
        var validatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;
        context.ValidationAudits.AddRange(
            new ValidationAudit
            {
                DispatchId = dispatch.Id,
                IsValid = true,
                Recommendation = "APPROVE",
                ValidatedAt = validatedAt,
            },
            new ValidationAudit
            {
                DispatchId = dispatch.Id,
                IsValid = false,
                Recommendation = "REJECT",
                ValidatedAt = validatedAt.AddTicks(1),
            });
        await context.SaveChangesAsync();

        var controller = new DispatchesController(context);
        var result = await controller.UpdateStatus(dispatch.Id, new UpdateDispatchStatusDto("Approved"));

        Assert.IsType<BadRequestObjectResult>(result);
        Assert.Equal("PendingApproval", dispatch.Status);
    }

BE-032 ERCP.Api.Tests.DispatchesControllerTests.UpdateTask_ChangesAssignmentAndRefreshesDispatchTimestamp

Passed

Responsible Reviewer
Denesh N
Source
SEF/emergency-resource-coordination-platform/backend-tests/DispatchesControllerTests.cs
Preconditions
Restored .NET 8 test project; fixtures created by the named test. Existing controller tests mostly use EF InMemory.
Steps
From the repository run dotnet test backend-tests/ERCP.Api.Tests.csproj --filter "FullyQualifiedName~ERCP.Api.Tests.DispatchesControllerTests.UpdateTask_ChangesAssignmentAndRefreshesDispatchTimestamp". Use the inline parameter shown in the case name when applicable.
Expected
Assert.IsType<NoContentResult>(result); Assert.Equal(nextVolunteer.Id, task.VolunteerId); Assert.Equal(resource.Id, task.ResourceId); Assert.Equal("Transport", task.TaskType); Assert.True(dispatch.UpdatedAt > previousUpdatedAt);
Actual
Passed; duration 00:00:00.0227540; recorded 2026-10-06T00:15:35.3480645+05:30
Evidence
evidence/final/backend/backend.trx
Fixture inputs and assertion source
public async Task UpdateTask_ChangesAssignmentAndRefreshesDispatchTimestamp()
    {
        using var context = CreateContext();
        var firstVolunteer = new Volunteer { Name = "First", Email = "first@test.local" };
        var nextVolunteer = new Volunteer { Name = "Next", Email = "next@test.local" };
        var dispatch = new Dispatch
        {
            IncidentId = 12,
            Priority = "High",
            Status = "PendingApproval",
            UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow.AddMinutes(-1),
        };
        context.Volunteers.AddRange(firstVolunteer, nextVolunteer);
        context.Dispatches.Add(dispatch);
        await context.SaveChangesAsync();
        var task = new DispatchTask
        {
            DispatchId = dispatch.Id,
            VolunteerId = firstVolunteer.Id,
            TaskType = "Medical",
        };
        var resource = new Resource { Name = "Rescue Vehicle", ResourceType = "Vehicle" };
        context.DispatchTasks.Add(task);
        context.Resources.Add(resource);
        await context.SaveChangesAsync();
        var previousUpdatedAt = dispatch.UpdatedAt;

        var controller = new DispatchesController(context);
        var result = await controller.UpdateTask(
            dispatch.Id,
            task.Id,
            new UpdateDispatchTaskDto(nextVolunteer.Id, resource.Id, "Transport"));

        Assert.IsType<NoContentResult>(result);
        Assert.Equal(nextVolunteer.Id, task.VolunteerId);
        Assert.Equal(resource.Id, task.ResourceId);
        Assert.Equal("Transport", task.TaskType);
        Assert.True(dispatch.UpdatedAt > previousUpdatedAt);
    }

public async Task GetNearestShelters_OrdersSheltersByProximity()
    {
        var context = GetInMemoryContext();
        context.Shelters.Add(new Shelter
        {
            Name = "Far Shelter",
            Location = "Kandy",
            TotalCapacity = 200,
            CurrentOccupancy = 10,
            Status = "Active",
            Latitude = 7.2906,
            Longitude = 80.6337
        });

        context.Shelters.Add(new Shelter
        {
            Name = "Near Shelter",
            Location = "Colombo Fort",
            TotalCapacity = 100,
            CurrentOccupancy = 20,
            Status = "Active",
            Latitude = 6.9344,
            Longitude = 79.8428
        });

        await context.SaveChangesAsync();

        var service = new GeolocationService(context);
        var nearest = await service.GetNearestSheltersAsync(6.9271, 79.8612, limit: 2);

        Assert.Equal(2, nearest.Count);
        Assert.Equal("Near Shelter", nearest[0].Name);
        Assert.True(nearest[0].DistanceKm < nearest[1].DistanceKm);
    }

public async Task CreateIncident_Successfully_ReturnsCreatedAndLogsTimelineEvent()
    {
        // Arrange
        var context = GetInMemoryContext();
        var controller = CreateController(context);

        var dto = new CreateIncidentDto
        {
            Title = "Industrial Chemical Spill",
            Description = "Tanker leak near riverbank",
            Location = "Sector 4 Industrial Zone",
            Category = "Chemical",
            Priority = "High",
            AffectedCount = 25,
            ReporterContact = "Plant Supervisor",
            AutoGeneratePlan = false
        };

        // Act
        var result = await controller.CreateIncident(dto);

        // Assert

        Assert.Equal("Reported", incidentDetail.Status);

            .Where(e => e.IncidentId == incidentDetail.Id)
            .ToListAsync();

        Assert.NotEmpty(timelineEvents);
        Assert.Contains(timelineEvents, e => e.EventType == "Created");
    }

public async Task GeneratePlan_CreatesAndReturnsIncidentPlan_AndLogsTimelineEvent()

        var incident = new Incident
        {
            Title = "Wildfire Threat",
            Category = "Fire",
            Priority = "Critical",
            Location = "Pine Ridge Hill",
            AffectedCount = 50,
            Status = "Reported"
        };
        context.Incidents.Add(incident);
        await context.SaveChangesAsync();

        var controller = CreateController(context);
        var requestDto = new GeneratePlanRequestDto
        {
            CustomDirectives = "Focus on residential evacuation",
            FocusPhase = "Mass Evacuation"
        };

        // Act

        // Assert

        var planDto = Assert.IsType<IncidentPlanDto>(okResult.Value);

        Assert.Equal(incident.Id, planDto.IncidentId);
        Assert.NotEmpty(planDto.ActionSteps);

        var savedPlan = await context.IncidentPlans.FirstOrDefaultAsync(p => p.IncidentId == incident.Id);
        Assert.NotNull(savedPlan);

        // Verify PlanGenerated timeline event
        var timelineEvent = await context.IncidentTimelineEvents
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(e => e.IncidentId == incident.Id && e.EventType == "PlanGenerated");
        Assert.NotNull(timelineEvent);
    }

public async Task GetTimeline_ReturnsEventsInChronologicalOrder()
    {
        // Arrange
        var context = GetInMemoryContext();
        var incident = new Incident { Title = "Building Collapse", Status = "Reported" };
        context.Incidents.Add(incident);
        await context.SaveChangesAsync();

        var time1 = DateTime.UtcNow.AddMinutes(-30);
        var time2 = DateTime.UtcNow.AddMinutes(-10);

        context.IncidentTimelineEvents.Add(new IncidentTimelineEvent
        {
            IncidentId = incident.Id,
            EventType = "Created",
            Title = "Incident Reported",
            Timestamp = time1
        });

        context.IncidentTimelineEvents.Add(new IncidentTimelineEvent
        {
            IncidentId = incident.Id,
            EventType = "StatusUpdate",
            Title = "Incident Verified",
            Timestamp = time2
        });

        await context.SaveChangesAsync();

        var controller = CreateController(context);

        // Act
        var result = await controller.GetTimeline(incident.Id);

        // Assert
        var okResult = Assert.IsType<OkObjectResult>(result.Result);
        var events = Assert.IsAssignableFrom<IEnumerable<IncidentTimelineEventDto>>(okResult.Value).ToList();

        Assert.Equal(2, events.Count);
        Assert.True(events[0].Timestamp <= events[1].Timestamp);
        Assert.Equal("Incident Reported", events[0].Title);
        Assert.Equal("Incident Verified", events[1].Title);
    }

        var incident = new Incident
        {
            Title = "Power Substation Failure",
            Status = "Reported",
            Priority = "High",
            Location = "Substation 9"
        };
        context.Incidents.Add(incident);
        await context.SaveChangesAsync();

        var controller = CreateController(context);
        var updateDto = new UpdateIncidentDto
        {
            Status = "Resolved" // Illegal jump: Reported -> Resolved
        };

        // Act
        var result = await controller.UpdateIncident(incident.Id, updateDto);

        // Assert
        var badRequestResult = Assert.IsType<BadRequestObjectResult>(result);
        Assert.NotNull(badRequestResult.Value);

        // Ensure status in database was NOT changed
        var unchangedIncident = await context.Incidents.FindAsync(incident.Id);
        Assert.NotNull(unchangedIncident);
        Assert.Equal("Reported", unchangedIncident.Status);
    }

public async Task UpdateStatus_ValidTransition_ReportedToVerified_Succeeds()
    {
        // Arrange
        var context = GetInMemoryContext();
        var incident = new Incident
        {
            Title = "Pipeline Leak",
            Status = "Reported",
            Priority = "Medium",
            Location = "North Station"
        };
        context.Incidents.Add(incident);
        await context.SaveChangesAsync();

        var controller = CreateController(context);
        var updateDto = new UpdateIncidentDto
        {
            Status = "Verified",
            Note = "Verified on site by field officer"
        };

        // Act
        var result = await controller.UpdateIncident(incident.Id, updateDto);

        // Assert

        Assert.NotNull(updatedIncident);
        Assert.Equal("Verified", updatedIncident.Status);

        // Verify timeline logging
        var timeline = await context.IncidentTimelineEvents
            .Where(e => e.IncidentId == incident.Id)
            .ToListAsync();

        var statusEvent = timeline.FirstOrDefault(e => e.Title == "Incident Verified");
        Assert.NotNull(statusEvent);
        Assert.Equal("StatusUpdate", statusEvent.EventType);
    }

MIG-002 ERCP.Api.Tests.QmsMigrationTests.MIG02_IncidentMigration_RollbackPreservesResourceData

Passed

Responsible Reviewer
M.Thanusikan
Source
SEF/emergency-resource-coordination-platform/backend-tests/QmsMigrationTests.cs
Preconditions
QMS_MIGRATION_ADMIN points to the isolated localhost postgres admin database; fixture owns temporary databases.
Steps
From the repository run dotnet test backend-tests/ERCP.Api.Tests.csproj --filter "FullyQualifiedName~ERCP.Api.Tests.QmsMigrationTests.MIG02_IncidentMigration_RollbackPreservesResourceData". Use the inline parameter shown in the case name when applicable.
Expected
Assert.Equal(before, await Schema(test.Db)); Assert.Equal("Migration Shelter", (await test.Db.Shelters.SingleAsync()).Name); Assert.Equal(shelter.Id, (await test.Db.Resources.SingleAsync()).ShelterId); Assert.Equal(shelter.Id, (await test.Db.InventoryItems.SingleAsync()).ShelterId); Assert.Equal(1, await test.Db.Resources.CountAsync()); Assert.Equal(1, await test.Db.InventoryItems.CountAsync());

Evidence
evidence/final/backend/backend.trx
Fixture inputs and assertion source
public async Task MIG02_IncidentMigration_RollbackPreservesResourceData()
    {
        await using var test = await TestDatabase.Create();
        var migrator = test.Db.GetService<IMigrator>();
        await migrator.MigrateAsync(ResourceMigration);
        var shelter = new Shelter { Name = "Migration Shelter", TotalCapacity = 10 };
        test.Db.Shelters.Add(shelter); await test.Db.SaveChangesAsync();
        test.Db.Resources.Add(new Resource { Name = "Keep Resource", ShelterId = shelter.Id });
        test.Db.InventoryItems.Add(new InventoryItem { ItemName = "Keep Inventory", ShelterId = shelter.Id });
        await test.Db.SaveChangesAsync();
        var before = await Schema(test.Db);
        await migrator.MigrateAsync(IncidentMigration);
        await migrator.MigrateAsync(ResourceMigration);
        Assert.Equal(before, await Schema(test.Db));
        test.Db.ChangeTracker.Clear();
        Assert.Equal("Migration Shelter", (await test.Db.Shelters.SingleAsync()).Name);
        Assert.Equal(shelter.Id, (await test.Db.Resources.SingleAsync()).ShelterId);
        Assert.Equal(shelter.Id, (await test.Db.InventoryItems.SingleAsync()).ShelterId);
        await migrator.MigrateAsync();
        Assert.Equal(1, await test.Db.Resources.CountAsync());
        Assert.Equal(1, await test.Db.InventoryItems.CountAsync());
    }

    {

        var migrator = test.Db.GetService<IMigrator>();
        await migrator.MigrateAsync(PreviousMigration);

            VALUES ('Preserved incident','Sample data','Colombo','Reported','High',NOW(),NOW());

public async Task MIG04_ReapplyAndFullRollback_AreConsistent()
    {

        var migrator = test.Db.GetService<IMigrator>();
        await migrator.MigrateAsync();
        test.Db.Users.Add(new User { FullName = "Migration User", Email = "migration@test.local", PasswordHash = "fixture" });

public async Task DB01_DuplicateEmail_IsRejectedByPostgres()

public async Task DB03_DeletingVolunteer_CascadesShift()
    {
        await using var db = Open();
        await using var tx = await db.Database.BeginTransactionAsync();
        var volunteer = new Volunteer { Id = Guid.NewGuid(), Name = "Cascade Test", Email = $"{Guid.NewGuid()}@test.local" };
        var shift = new Shift { VolunteerId = volunteer.Id, StartTime = DateTime.UtcNow, EndTime = DateTime.UtcNow.AddHours(1) };

From the repository run dotnet test backend-tests/ERCP.Api.Tests.csproj --filter "FullyQualifiedName~ERCP.Api.Tests.QmsPostgresTests.DB04_Rollback_DoesNotPersistResource". Use the inline parameter shown in the case name when applicable.

Evidence
evidence/final/backend/backend.trx
Fixture inputs and assertion source
public async Task DB04_Rollback_DoesNotPersistResource()
    {
        var name = $"Rollback-{Guid.NewGuid()}";
        await using (var db = Open())
        {
            await using var tx = await db.Database.BeginTransactionAsync();
            db.Resources.Add(new Resource { Name = name }); await db.SaveChangesAsync(); await tx.RollbackAsync();
        }
        await using var verify = Open();
        Assert.False(await verify.Resources.AnyAsync(r => r.Name == name));
    }
}

public async Task GetAvailable_ReturnsOnlyAvailableResources()
    {

        context.Resources.Add(new Resource { Name = "R1", Status = "Available" });
        context.Resources.Add(new Resource { Name = "R2", Status = "Dispatched" });
        context.Resources.Add(new Resource { Name = "R3", Status = "Maintenance" });
        await context.SaveChangesAsync();

        var controller = new ResourcesController(context);

        var resources = Assert.IsAssignableFrom<IEnumerable<ResourceResponseDto>>(okResult.Value);

public async Task AssessSituation_GeneratesValidAssessment()
    {
        var context = GetInMemoryContext();
        var incident = new Incident
        {
            Title = "Major River Flood & Medical Emergency",
            Description = "Rising water level and multiple casualties reported.",
            Location = "District 4",
            Priority = "Critical"
        };
        context.Incidents.Add(incident);

        context.Shelters.Add(new Shelter
        {
            Name = "High School Evacuation Hub",
            TotalCapacity = 300,
            CurrentOccupancy = 50,
            Status = "Active"
        });

        await context.SaveChangesAsync();

        var service = new SituationAnalysisService(context);
        var controller = new SituationAnalysisController(service);

        var result = await controller.AssessSituation(new AssessSituationRequestDto { IncidentId = incident.Id });

        var okResult = Assert.IsType<Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc.OkObjectResult>(result.Result);
        var assessment = Assert.IsType<SituationAssessmentResponseDto>(okResult.Value);

        Assert.True(assessment.UrgencyScore >= 85);
        Assert.Equal("Critical", assessment.UrgencyLevel);
        Assert.Contains("Nearest shelter identified", assessment.AnalysisSummary);
        Assert.NotNull(assessment.NearestShelterName);
    }
}

public async Task UpdateShift_ValidWindow_UpdatesStatus()
    {
        var ctx = GetCtx();
        var v = new Volunteer { Name = "N", Email = "e@x.com", Phone = "1", Skills = "s" };
        ctx.Volunteers.Add(v);
        await ctx.SaveChangesAsync();
        var s = new Shift { VolunteerId = v.Id, StartTime = DateTime.UtcNow, EndTime = DateTime.UtcNow.AddHours(4), Status = ShiftStatus.Scheduled };
        ctx.Shifts.Add(s);
        await ctx.SaveChangesAsync();

        var c = new VolunteersController(ctx);
        var res = await c.UpdateShift(v.Id, s.Id, new UpdateShiftDto
        {
            StartTime = s.StartTime,
            EndTime = s.EndTime.AddHours(1),
            Status = "Active"
        });

        var ok = Assert.IsType<OkObjectResult>(res.Result);
        var updated = Assert.IsType<ShiftResponseDto>(ok.Value);
        Assert.Equal(ShiftStatus.Active.ToString(), updated.Status);
    }

evidence/final/backend/backend.trx
Fixture inputs and assertion source
public async Task GetTasks_ReturnsTasksWithDispatchAndIncidentContext()
    {
        var (ctx, volunteer) = await SeedVolunteerAsync();
        var incident = new Incident
        {
            Title = "Warehouse fire",
            Description = "desc",
            Location = "Sector 4",
            Category = "Fire",
            Priority = "High",
            Status = "Reported",
        };
        ctx.Incidents.Add(incident);
        await ctx.SaveChangesAsync();

        var dispatch = new Dispatch { IncidentId = incident.Id, Priority = "High", Status = "PendingApproval" };
        ctx.Dispatches.Add(dispatch);
        await ctx.SaveChangesAsync();

        var resource = new Resource
        {
            Name = "Ambulance 1",
            ResourceType = "Vehicle",
            Status = "Available",
            Capacity = 4,
            Location = "Depot",
        };
        ctx.Resources.Add(resource);
        await ctx.SaveChangesAsync();

        ctx.DispatchTasks.Add(new DispatchTask
        {
            DispatchId = dispatch.Id,
            VolunteerId = volunteer.Id,
            ResourceId = resource.Id,
            TaskType = "Medical response",
            Status = "Assigned",
            AssignedAt = DateTime.UtcNow,
        });
        await ctx.SaveChangesAsync();

        var controller = new VolunteersController(ctx);
        var result = await controller.GetTasks(volunteer.Id);

        var ok = Assert.IsType<Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc.OkObjectResult>(result.Result);
        var tasks = Assert.IsAssignableFrom<IEnumerable<VolunteerTaskDto>>(ok.Value);
        var task = Assert.Single(tasks);
        Assert.Equal(dispatch.Id, task.DispatchId);
        Assert.Equal(incident.Id, task.IncidentId);
        Assert.Equal("Warehouse fire", task.IncidentTitle);
        Assert.Equal("PendingApproval", task.DispatchStatus);
        Assert.Equal("Medical response", task.TaskType);
        Assert.Equal("Ambulance 1", task.ResourceName);
    }

public async Task CreateShift_InvalidWindow_OverHttp_Returns400()
    {
        var client = _factory.CreateClient();
        var token = await RegisterCoordinatorAsync(client);
        client.DefaultRequestHeaders.Authorization = new AuthenticationHeaderValue("Bearer", token);

        var create = await client.PostAsJsonAsync("/api/volunteers", new
        {
            name = "E2E Window",
            email = UniqueEmail("e2e-window"),
            phone = "1",
            skills = "driving",
        });
        using var createdDoc = JsonDocument.Parse(await create.Content.ReadAsStringAsync());
        var volunteerId = createdDoc.RootElement.GetProperty("id").GetGuid();

        var badShift = await client.PostAsJsonAsync($"/api/volunteers/{volunteerId}/shifts", new
        {
            startTime = new DateTime(2026, 10, 3, 16, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc),
            endTime = new DateTime(2026, 10, 3, 8, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc),
            status = "Scheduled",
        });

        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.BadRequest, badShift.StatusCode);
    }
}

public async Task VolunteerShiftLifecycle_OverHttp()
    {
        var client = _factory.CreateClient();
        var token = await RegisterCoordinatorAsync(client);
        client.DefaultRequestHeaders.Authorization = new AuthenticationHeaderValue("Bearer", token);

        // 1. Register a volunteer
        var email = UniqueEmail("e2e-responder");
        var create = await client.PostAsJsonAsync("/api/volunteers", new
        {
            name = "E2E Responder",
            email,
            phone = "0770000000",
            skills = "medical, rescue",
        });
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.Created, create.StatusCode);
        using var createdDoc = JsonDocument.Parse(await create.Content.ReadAsStringAsync());
        var volunteerId = createdDoc.RootElement.GetProperty("id").GetGuid();
        Assert.NotEqual(Guid.Empty, volunteerId);

        // 2. Assign a shift
        var start = new DateTime(2026, 10, 2, 8, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc);
        var end = new DateTime(2026, 10, 2, 16, 0, 0, DateTimeKind.Utc);
        var shift = await client.PostAsJsonAsync($"/api/volunteers/{volunteerId}/shifts", new
        {
            startTime = start,
            endTime = end,
            status = "Scheduled",
        });
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.Created, shift.StatusCode);

        // 3. Shift history contains the new shift
        var history = await client.GetAsync($"/api/volunteers/{volunteerId}/shifts");
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.OK, history.StatusCode);
        var historyBody = await history.Content.ReadAsStringAsync();
        Assert.Contains("2026-10-02", historyBody);

        // 4. Toggle availability off-duty and verify via GET
        var toggle = await client.PutAsJsonAsync(
            $"/api/volunteers/{volunteerId}/availability", new { isAvailable = false });
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.NoContent, toggle.StatusCode);

        var fetched = await client.GetAsync($"/api/volunteers/{volunteerId}");
        Assert.Equal(HttpStatusCode.OK, fetched.StatusCode);
        using var fetchedDoc = JsonDocument.Parse(await fetched.Content.ReadAsStringAsync());
        Assert.False(fetchedDoc.RootElement.GetProperty("isAvailable").GetBoolean());
    }

public async Task UpdateAvailability_CoordinatorCanUpdateAnyVolunteer_ReturnsNoContent()
    {
        // Arrange
        var context = GetInMemoryContext();
        var volunteer = new Volunteer { Name = "Any Person", Email = "any@example.com", Phone = "123", Skills = "driving", IsAvailable = false };
        context.Volunteers.Add(volunteer);
        await context.SaveChangesAsync();

        var controller = new VolunteersController(context);
        var claims = new[]
        {
            new System.Security.Claims.Claim(System.Security.Claims.ClaimTypes.Email, "coordinator@system.local"),
            new System.Security.Claims.Claim(System.Security.Claims.ClaimTypes.Role, "Coordinator")
        };
        var identity = new System.Security.Claims.ClaimsIdentity(claims, "TestAuth");
        controller.ControllerContext = new Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc.ControllerContext
        {
            HttpContext = new Microsoft.AspNetCore.Http.DefaultHttpContext { User = new System.Security.Claims.ClaimsPrincipal(identity) }
        };

        // Act
        var result = await controller.UpdateAvailability(volunteer.Id, new UpdateAvailabilityDto { IsAvailable = true });

        // Assert
        Assert.IsType<Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc.NoContentResult>(result);
        var updated = await context.Volunteers.FindAsync(volunteer.Id);
        Assert.True(updated!.IsAvailable);
    }
}

    renderPage();

    fireEvent.change(screen.getByPlaceholderText("e.g. Amara Perera"), {
      target: { value: "Amara Perera" },
    });
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByPlaceholderText("amara@email.com"), {

WEB-015 DispatchList coordinator workflow lets coordinators adjust a task before validating and approving

Passed

Responsible Reviewer
Denesh N
Source
SEF/emergency-resource-coordination-platform/frontend-react/src/pages/__tests__/DispatchList.test.jsx
Preconditions
Vitest jsdom environment with test setup and service mocks; no live browser or server is required.
Steps
In frontend-react run npm test -- DispatchList.test.jsx -t "lets coordinators adjust a task before validating and approving". Test body supplies inputs and UI interactions.
Expected
expect(dispatchService.updateDispatchTask).toHaveBeenCalledWith(5, 22, { expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Approve Proposal" })).toBeEnabled(); expect(dispatchService.updateDispatchStatus).toHaveBeenCalledWith(5, "Approved");
Actual
passed; 298.56 ms
Evidence
evidence/final/react.json
Fixture inputs and assertion source
it("lets coordinators adjust a task before validating and approving", async () => {
    validationService.validateDispatch.mockResolvedValue({
      isValid: true,
      recommendation: "APPROVE",
      checks: [{ tool: "check_volunteer_availability", passed: true, detail: "Available" }],
      violations: [],
      warnings: [],
    });
    dispatchService.updateDispatchTask.mockResolvedValue({});
    dispatchService.updateDispatchStatus.mockResolvedValue({});
    renderPage();

    await screen.findByText("Evacuation allocation");
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Edit" }));

    const [volunteerSelect, resourceSelect] = screen.getAllByRole("combobox");
    fireEvent.change(volunteerSelect, { target: { value: "vol-2" } });
    fireEvent.change(resourceSelect, { target: { value: "4" } });
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByPlaceholderText("e.g. Medical Response, Transport"), { target: { value: "Transport" } });
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Save Assignment" }));

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(dispatchService.updateDispatchTask).toHaveBeenCalledWith(5, 22, {
        volunteerId: "vol-2",
        resourceId: 4,
        taskType: "Transport",
      });
    });

    fireEvent.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "Run Safety Check" }));
    await screen.findByTestId("validation-APPROVE");
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Approve Proposal" })).toBeEnabled();
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Approve Proposal" }));
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(dispatchService.updateDispatchStatus).toHaveBeenCalledWith(5, "Approved");
    });
  });

WEB-019 InventoryList Component should identify low stock items based on minimum threshold

Passed

Responsible Reviewer
M.Thanusikan
Source
SEF/emergency-resource-coordination-platform/frontend-react/src/pages/__tests__/InventoryList.test.jsx
Preconditions
Vitest jsdom environment with test setup and service mocks; no live browser or server is required.
Steps
In frontend-react run npm test -- InventoryList.test.jsx -t "should identify low stock items based on minimum threshold". Test body supplies inputs and UI interactions.
Expected
expect(checkStatus(5, 10)).toBe('LowStock'); expect(checkStatus(0, 10)).toBe('OutOfStock'); expect(checkStatus(20, 10)).toBe('InStock');
Actual
passed; 0.96 ms
Evidence
evidence/final/react.json
Fixture inputs and assertion source
it('should identify low stock items based on minimum threshold', () => {
    const checkStatus = (qty, threshold) => qty === 0 ? 'OutOfStock' : qty <= threshold ? 'LowStock' : 'InStock';
    expect(checkStatus(5, 10)).toBe('LowStock');
    expect(checkStatus(0, 10)).toBe('OutOfStock');
    expect(checkStatus(20, 10)).toBe('InStock');
  });
});

Expected

Actual
passed; 0.95 ms
Evidence
evidence/final/react.json
Fixture inputs and assertion source
it('should filter available resources correctly', () => {
    const resources = [
      { id: 1, name: 'Ambulance 1', status: 'Available' },
      { id: 2, name: 'Truck 2', status: 'Dispatched' }
    ];
    const available = resources.filter(r => r.status === 'Available');
    expect(available.length).toBe(1);
    expect(available[0].name).toBe('Ambulance 1');
  });
});

WEB-033 VolunteerDetail (Student 4 shift tracking) shows Assign Shift only to coordinators and creates a shift on submit

Passed

Responsible Reviewer
Deneshkar. P
Source
SEF/emergency-resource-coordination-platform/frontend-react/src/pages/__tests__/VolunteerDetail.test.jsx
Preconditions
Vitest jsdom environment with test setup and service mocks; no live browser or server is required.
Steps
In frontend-react run npm test -- VolunteerDetail.test.jsx -t "shows Assign Shift only to coordinators and creates a shift on submit". Test body supplies inputs and UI interactions.
Expected
await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByText("No shifts recorded")).toBeInTheDocument()); expect(screen.getByRole("heading", { name: "Assign Shift" })).toBeInTheDocument(); await waitFor(() => expect(volunteerService.createShift).toHaveBeenCalled()); expect(callId).toBe(VOLUNTEER_ID); expect(body.status).toBe("Scheduled"); expect(new Date(body.endTime) > new Date(body.startTime)).toBe(true); expect(volunteerService.getVolunteerShifts).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
Actual
passed; 271.14 ms
Evidence
evidence/final/react.json
Fixture inputs and assertion source
it("shows Assign Shift only to coordinators and creates a shift on submit", async () => {
    mockCoordinator();
    volunteerService.getVolunteerShifts.mockResolvedValue([]);
    volunteerService.createShift.mockResolvedValue({ id: 9 });

    renderPage();
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByText("No shifts recorded")).toBeInTheDocument());

    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText("+ Assign Shift"));
    expect(screen.getByRole("heading", { name: "Assign Shift" })).toBeInTheDocument();

    const [startInput, endInput] = screen.getAllByDisplayValue("");
    fireEvent.change(startInput, { target: { value: "2026-09-21T08:00" } });
    fireEvent.change(endInput, { target: { value: "2026-09-21T16:00" } });
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText("Assign Shift", { selector: "button[type='submit']" }));

    await waitFor(() => expect(volunteerService.createShift).toHaveBeenCalled());
    const [callId, body] = volunteerService.createShift.mock.calls[0];
    expect(callId).toBe(VOLUNTEER_ID);
    expect(body.status).toBe("Scheduled");
    expect(new Date(body.endTime) > new Date(body.startTime)).toBe(true);
    // List refreshes after a successful assign
    expect(volunteerService.getVolunteerShifts).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
  });

WEB-045 validationService canApprove requires a valid APPROVE result with at least one check

Passed

Responsible Reviewer
Deneshkar. P
Source
SEF/emergency-resource-coordination-platform/frontend-react/src/services/__tests__/validationService.test.js
Preconditions
Vitest jsdom environment with test setup and service mocks; no live browser or server is required.
Steps
In frontend-react run npm test -- validationService.test.js -t "canApprove requires a valid APPROVE result with at least one check". Test body supplies inputs and UI interactions.
Expected
expect(canApprove({ isValid: true, recommendation: "APPROVE", checks: [{ tool: "t", passed: true }] })).toBe(true); expect(canApprove({ isValid: true, recommendation: "REVISE", checks: [{ tool: "t", passed: true }] })).toBe(false); expect(canApprove({ isValid: true, recommendation: "APPROVE", checks: [] })).toBe(false); expect(canApprove({ isValid: false, recommendation: "APPROVE", checks: [{ tool: "t", passed: true }] })).toBe(false); expect(canApprove({ isValid: false, recommendation: "REJECT", checks: [{ tool: "t", passed: false }] })).toBe(false); expect(canApprove(null)).toBe(false); expect(canApprove(undefined)).toBe(false);
Actual
passed; 1.36 ms
Evidence
evidence/final/react.json
Fixture inputs and assertion source
it("canApprove requires a valid APPROVE result with at least one check", () => {
    expect(canApprove({ isValid: true, recommendation: "APPROVE", checks: [{ tool: "t", passed: true }] })).toBe(true);
    expect(canApprove({ isValid: true, recommendation: "REVISE", checks: [{ tool: "t", passed: true }] })).toBe(false);
    // Empty (0-check) results never approve — even with APPROVE verdict
    expect(canApprove({ isValid: true, recommendation: "APPROVE", checks: [] })).toBe(false);
    expect(canApprove({ isValid: false, recommendation: "APPROVE", checks: [{ tool: "t", passed: true }] })).toBe(false);
    expect(canApprove({ isValid: false, recommendation: "REJECT", checks: [{ tool: "t", passed: false }] })).toBe(false);
    expect(canApprove(null)).toBe(false);
    expect(canApprove(undefined)).toBe(false);
  });

MOB-013 InventoryItemModel stock adjustment scenario: quantity decreases from 50 to 10

Passed

Responsible Reviewer
M.Thanusikan
Source
SEF/emergency-resource-coordination-platform/mobile-flutter/test/models/inventory_item_model_test.dart
Preconditions
Flutter dependencies installed; flutter_test binding and mock preferences initialized by the test fixture.
Steps
In mobile-flutter run flutter test "test\models\inventory_item_model_test.dart" --plain-name "InventoryItemModel stock adjustment scenario: quantity decreases from 50 to 10". Use the fixture and widget interactions shown in source.
Expected
expect(before.quantity, 50); expect(before.status, 'InStock'); expect(after.quantity, 10); expect(after.status, 'LowStock'); expect(after.quantity < after.minimumThreshold, isTrue);
Actual
Flutter testDone result=success; skipped=False; stream completed success=true.
Evidence
evidence/final/flutter.jsonl
Fixture inputs and assertion source
test('stock adjustment scenario: quantity decreases from 50 to 10', () {
      // Simulates the backend AdjustStock test case
      final before = InventoryItemModel.fromJson({
        'id': 7,
        'itemName': 'Bottled Water',
        'category': 'Water',
        'quantity': 50,
        'minimumThreshold': 20,
        'status': 'InStock',
      });

      expect(before.quantity, 50);
      expect(before.status, 'InStock');

      // After backend adjustment of -40
      final after = InventoryItemModel.fromJson({
        'id': 7,
        'itemName': 'Bottled Water',
        'category': 'Water',
        'quantity': 10,
        'minimumThreshold': 20,
        'status': 'LowStock',
      });

      expect(after.quantity, 10);
      expect(after.status, 'LowStock');
      expect(after.quantity < after.minimumThreshold, isTrue);
    });
  });
}

MOB-020 AuthUser model fromJson -> toJson -> fromJson round-trip preserves data

Passed

Responsible Reviewer
Jathurshy.S
Source
SEF/emergency-resource-coordination-platform/mobile-flutter/test/models/auth_user_model_test.dart
Preconditions
Flutter dependencies installed; flutter_test binding and mock preferences initialized by the test fixture.
Steps
In mobile-flutter run flutter test "test\models\auth_user_model_test.dart" --plain-name "AuthUser model fromJson -> toJson -> fromJson round-trip preserves data". Use the fixture and widget interactions shown in source.
Expected
expect(user2.id, user1.id); expect(user2.fullName, user1.fullName); expect(user2.email, user1.email); expect(user2.role, user1.role); expect(user2.phoneNumber, user1.phoneNumber); expect(user2.skills, user1.skills);
Actual
Flutter testDone result=success; skipped=False; stream completed success=true.
Evidence
evidence/final/flutter.jsonl
Fixture inputs and assertion source
test('fromJson -> toJson -> fromJson round-trip preserves data', () {
      final original = {
        'id': 99,
        'fullName': 'Consistency Check',
        'email': 'consistency@ercp.org',
        'role': 'Coordinator',
        'phoneNumber': '+94770000000',
        'skills': 'Planning, Logistics',
      };

      final user1 = AuthUser.fromJson(original);
      final json = user1.toJson();
      final user2 = AuthUser.fromJson(json);

      expect(user2.id, user1.id);
      expect(user2.fullName, user1.fullName);
      expect(user2.email, user1.email);
      expect(user2.role, user1.role);
      expect(user2.phoneNumber, user1.phoneNumber);
      expect(user2.skills, user1.skills);
    });
  });
}

test('login with invalid credentials sets errorMessage', () async {
      SharedPreferences.setMockInitialValues({});
      final auth = AuthProvider();
      await auth.init();

      // This will fail because no backend is running, which tests error handling
      final result = await auth.login('invalid@test.org', 'wrongpassword');

evidence/final/flutter.jsonl
Fixture inputs and assertion source
test('register with missing backend sets errorMessage', () async {
      SharedPreferences.setMockInitialValues({});
      final auth = AuthProvider();
      await auth.init();

      final result = await auth.register(
        fullName: 'Test',
        email: 'test@ercp.org',
        password: 'test123',
        role: 'Citizen',
      );

      expect(result, isFalse);
      expect(auth.errorMessage, isNotNull);
      expect(auth.isLoading, isFalse);
    });

MOB-050 AuthProvider multiple logout calls are idempotent

Passed

Responsible Reviewer
Jathurshy.S
Source
SEF/emergency-resource-coordination-platform/mobile-flutter/test/providers/auth_provider_test.dart
Preconditions
Flutter dependencies installed; flutter_test binding and mock preferences initialized by the test fixture.
Steps
In mobile-flutter run flutter test "test\providers\auth_provider_test.dart" --plain-name "AuthProvider multiple logout calls are idempotent". Use the fixture and widget interactions shown in source.
Expected
expect(auth.user, isNull); expect(auth.token, isNull); expect(auth.isAuthenticated, isFalse);
Actual
Flutter testDone result=success; skipped=False; stream completed success=true.
Evidence
evidence/final/flutter.jsonl
Fixture inputs and assertion source
test('multiple logout calls are idempotent', () async {
      SharedPreferences.setMockInitialValues({});
      final auth = AuthProvider();
      await auth.init();

      await auth.logout();
      await auth.logout();
      await auth.logout();

      expect(auth.user, isNull);
      expect(auth.token, isNull);
      expect(auth.isAuthenticated, isFalse);
    });

MOB-051 AuthProvider notifyListeners is called during login (loading transitions)

Passed

Responsible Reviewer
Jathurshy.S
Source
SEF/emergency-resource-coordination-platform/mobile-flutter/test/providers/auth_provider_test.dart
Preconditions
Flutter dependencies installed; flutter_test binding and mock preferences initialized by the test fixture.
Steps
In mobile-flutter run flutter test "test\providers\auth_provider_test.dart" --plain-name "AuthProvider notifyListeners is called during login (loading transitions)". Use the fixture and widget interactions shown in source.
Expected
expect(notifyCount, greaterThanOrEqualTo(2));
Actual
Flutter testDone result=success; skipped=False; stream completed success=true.
Evidence
evidence/final/flutter.jsonl
Fixture inputs and assertion source
test('notifyListeners is called during login (loading transitions)', () async {
      SharedPreferences.setMockInitialValues({});
      final auth = AuthProvider();
      await auth.init();

      int notifyCount = 0;
      auth.addListener(() => notifyCount++);

      await auth.login('test@ercp.org', 'password123');

      // At minimum, loading=true and loading=false transitions
      expect(notifyCount, greaterThanOrEqualTo(2));
    });
  });
}

testWidgets('empty submit shows validation errors, does not call backend', (tester) async {
    await _pumpRegister(tester);

    await tester.ensureVisible(find.textContaining('Register as'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    await tester.tap(find.textContaining('Register as'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();

    // Form validators must fire for the three required fields.
    expect(find.text('Full name is required'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('Email is required'), findsOneWidget);

HTTP-001 test_E2E01_incident_plan_dispatch_validation_approval_persistence

Passed

Responsible Reviewer
Deneshkar. P
Source
QMS/scripts/test_live_api.py
Preconditions
Local API on 127.0.0.1:5009, isolated PostgreSQL, seeded coordinator, outbound email disabled.
Steps
From assignment root run python -m pytest QMS/scripts/test_live_api.py -k "test_E2E01_incident_plan_dispatch_validation_approval_persistence" -v. Parameter values are included in the case name.
Expected
assert incident.status_code == 201, incident.text assert plan.status_code in (200,201), plan.text assert volunteer.status_code == 201, volunteer.text assert dispatch.status_code == 201, dispatch.text assert call('PUT',f'/api/dispatches/{dispatch_id}/status',coordinator,json={'status':'Approved'}).status_code == 400 assert task.status_code == 200, task.text assert validation.status_code == 200, validation.text assert validation.json()['isValid'] is True, validation.text
Actual
JUnit records pass; 0.952 seconds.
Evidence
evidence/final/live-api.xml
Fixture inputs and assertion source
def test_E2E01_incident_plan_dispatch_validation_approval_persistence(coordinator):
    token, email = citizen()
    incident = call('POST','/api/incident',token,json={'title':'Warehouse fire emergency','description':'Smoke is spreading through the main storage building and people need rescue.',
        'location':'Colombo Main Road','category':'Fire','priority':'High','affectedCount':5,'reporterContact':email,'autoGeneratePlan':False})
    assert incident.status_code == 201, incident.text
    incident_id = incident.json()['id']
    plan = call('POST',f'/api/incident/{incident_id}/generate-plan',coordinator,json={})
    assert plan.status_code in (200,201), plan.text
    volunteer = call('POST','/api/volunteers',coordinator,json={'name':'QMS Responder','email':f'{uuid.uuid4().hex}@test.local','phone':'0770000000','skills':'medical, rescue'})
    assert volunteer.status_code == 201, volunteer.text
    volunteer_id = volunteer.json()['id']
    dispatch = call('POST','/api/dispatches',coordinator,json={'incidentId':incident_id,'priority':'High'})
    assert dispatch.status_code == 201, dispatch.text
    dispatch_id = dispatch.json()['id']
    # Approval must fail before safety validation.
    assert call('PUT',f'/api/dispatches/{dispatch_id}/status',coordinator,json={'status':'Approved'}).status_code == 400
    task = call('POST',f'/api/dispatches/{dispatch_id}/tasks',coordinator,json={'volunteerId':volunteer_id,'taskType':'Rescue'})
    assert task.status_code == 200, task.text
    validation = call('POST',f'/api/validations/dispatch/{dispatch_id}',coordinator,json={})
    assert validation.status_code == 200, validation.text
    assert validation.json()['isValid'] is True, validation.text
    assert validation.json()['recommendation'] == 'APPROVE'
    approved = call('PUT',f'/api/dispatches/{dispatch_id}/status',coordinator,json={'status':'Approved'})
    assert approved.status_code == 200, approved.text
    saved = call('GET',f'/api/dispatches/{dispatch_id}',coordinator)
    assert saved.json()['status'] == 'Approved'
    assert saved.json()['tasks'][0]['volunteerId'] == volunteer_id
    # Locked tasks must stay immutable after approval.
    assert call('POST',f'/api/dispatches/{dispatch_id}/tasks',coordinator,json={'volunteerId':volunteer_id,'taskType':'Medical'}).status_code == 409
    result = {'incident_id':incident_id,'plan_id':plan.json().get('id'),'dispatch_id':dispatch_id,
        'volunteer_id':volunteer_id,'validation':validation.json(),'saved_dispatch':saved.json(),
        'components':['HTTP client','ASP.NET Core','planner integration','safety service','PostgreSQL'],
        'scope':'API integration workflow; not a browser or mobile E2E test'}
    out = Path(os.environ.get('QMS_WORKFLOW_OUTPUT', str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / 'evidence/final/workflow.json')))
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    out.write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
